Let's outline code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math, math, sys

def pad_size(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return p

def fft_conv_full(a):  # length 2n-1
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([],float)
    size=1
    while size<2*n-1: size*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
    return c[:2*n-1]

def fast_score(a):
    n=len(a); S=a.sum()
    if S<0.01: return inf
    c=fft_conv_full(a)
    return float(2.0*n*c.max()/(S*S))
```
But for gradient, need c and r.

def soft_gradient(a, beta, beta1? no, return grad, score_soft, cmax)

    dmax=d.max()
    # weights
    x=beta*(d-dmax)
    x-=x.max() # dmax already 0
    w=np.exp(x/scale?)

def optimize_sequence(a, deadline, seed):
   n=len(a)
   a=normalize(a,n)
   best_score=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
   best_a=a.copy()
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   beta=20
   max_beta=500

   no_improve=0; eval_every=10

      grad, ssoft = soft_gradient(a,beta)
      # maybe if ssoft > best+? reset? not
      t+=1
      m=0.9*m+0.1*grad
      v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
      a_new = a - lr * m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
      a_new=np.clip(a_new,0,None)
      a=normalize(a_new,n)
      if t%eval_every==0:
          sc=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
          if sc<best_score: best... no_improve=0; print
          else no_improve+=1
      # schedule beta
      if t%200==0 and beta<max_beta: beta*=1.2
      if no_improve>?? adapt lr/restart

Let's design final algorithm around **multiplicative soft-max updates**, multiple starts, lengths, and final coordinate descent.

Detailed plan:
1. Set deadline = time.time() + 2400 (or use passed `budget_s`? The propose_candidate may be called with kwargs; we can use time limit constant 2350 to be safe). The function should return before.
2. Get parent `height_sequence_1` if available; clip, normalize.
3. Decide lengths to try: maybe [2535? parent length, 4096, 8192]. Time allocation: if start from parent, can try same length first to ensure not worse (1.519). Then larger lengths with seeds. But gradient with length 8192 may take time but enough.
   - We can create seed shapes for each length using a `make_seed(length, seed)` that blends parent (resampled) and parametric sweeps.
   - Use faster `fast_score` to select best seed for each length, maybe exact for a few.
4. Optimization function `optimize(a, deadline, beta_start, beta_end, seed)` returns best exact sequence and score.
   - Internal soft-multiplicative loop.
   - Periodically exact evaluate (`evaluate_sequence`) if fast hard score is lower than current best by margin or every N.
   - Use restarts from best with random multiplicative perturbations if stuck. 
5. After time, return best_a global across all optimizations.

Let's specify soft update function:
```
def soft_update(a, beta, temp=0.0):
    n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    c=fft_conv_full(a)
    c = np.maximum(c,0)
    d = c / n
    dmax = d.max()
    # For very large dmax? O1
    x = beta*(d - dmax)
    # to avoid all zeros if underflow, max is 0
    # if x too negative, exp zero. OK.
    e = np.exp(x)
    sume=e.sum()
    if sume <=0 or not finite: e=np.ones(L); e[int(np.argmax(d))]=1; sume=1
    w=e/sume
    # score soft = 2*(dmax + (1/beta)*(log(sume)-log(L))) if beta>0 else 2*dmax
    # r
    W_rev=w[::-1]
    size=1
    while size < n + L - 1: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(W_rev, size)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, size)
    r = h[L-1 : L-n : -1]   # length n? Check slice: stop L-n, step -1. If L=5,n=3, L-n=2, start4 stop2 -> indices 4,3 length2? Need include L-n+1? Let's test: want i=0..n-1, m=L-1-i: for i=n-1, m=L-n. Need slice from L-1 down to L-n inclusive. Python `h[L-1:L-n-1:-1]`? Example L=5,n=3: indices desired 4,3,2. `h[4:1:-1]` gives 4,3,2. So `h[L-1 : L-n : -1]` gives 4,3? because stop 2 exclusive => 4,3. Need stop `L-n-1`. If L-n >=0. So r=h[L-1 : L-n-1 : -1]. But if L-n-1 negative? L=2n-1, L-n-1=n-2 >=0 for n≥2. For n=1 handle. So r=h[L-1 : L-n-1 : -1].
    # normalize r, clip small negative
    r=np.maximum(r,0.0)
    # Update direction
    r_mean=r.mean()
    r_center=r - r_mean
    # optional standardize for step
    # return r_center, dmax, soft_score, h? cmax=n*dmax
```
Gradient direction should be negative of raw r? We'll define `direction = r_center`. Then update `a *= exp(-lr*direction)` if direction positive high contribution -> reduce. If direction negative -> increase. Standardize direction to unit std to control_lr:
```
sigma = r_center.std()
if sigma>1e-8: r_update = r_center/sigma else zero
```
Then `a *= exp(-lr * r_update)`. `lr` maybe 0.3. This makes relative changes based on standardized contribution. But if differences small, may introduce zero-mean noise; maybe okay with momentum. To avoid noise, only std if above threshold. Use `scale = np.mean(np.abs(r_center))+1e-8; r_update = r_center/scale`.

Could also include a small uniform weight always: add `w = (w + 1/L) / (1+1)`? But hard max required. Maybe use `beta` schedule high.

Adam in log multiplicative: Keep velocity `v` of standardized r_update. Update:
```

a=normalize(a,n)
```
If lr=0.2, v~0.1, exp changes ~0.2. Good. Let's use initial lr=0.1, adaptive.

However standardization each iteration can cause tiny fluctuations to dominate when near optimum. Instead scale direction by `np.percentile(np.abs(r_center),95)` or by current step target. Use `scale = np.max(np.abs(r_center))`; if scale>1e-6 use. Then r_update in [-1,1]. lr 0.05. This is safe.

Line search / acceptance: We can maintain `fast_metric` =2*cmax/n from hard max. Since update may increase cmax. We can after update compute new c? We already computed old c; to evaluate new, need new convolve. But each iteration computes convolve for gradient; we can after update compute new fast score in same loop? That doubles FFT? We can compute update using old r, then next iteration compute c_new; if score worse, we could rollback? Need store a_old. We can accept always, nonmonotonic maybe. To be safe, after update compute new `c_new = fft_conv_full(a)`? That's one extra FFT. But next iteration would anyway; we can keep c from current for next, not evaluate new until next. Better: Use two-step: compute old c. After update, compute new c (fast) and if new_cmax significantly larger than old_cmax (e.g., > old*1.1), revert to old and reduce lr. This costs one extra conv but gradient c for next is new. We can avoid computing c twice by computing old c for gradient, update, then compute new c for acceptance and store for next. The next gradient would need c anyway; so no extra if we replace old c with new c. But update computation already uses old weights from old c; okay. Loop:
```
c = fft(a)  # initial or from prev
for:
   weights, r from c
   a_old=a.copy()
   update a
   c_new=fft(a_new)
   if 2*c_new.max()/n > min_score + margin? decide accept or reject
   if accept: a=a_new; c=c_new; update best fast
   else: a=a_old; c=c_old; lr*=0.7
```
This uses one FFT per accepted/rejected iteration (old c is previous). When reject, need c_old stored. Good. Initial one FFT.

But weights/r computation also uses FFT to compute r, plus c FFT. That's two FFT transforms per iteration (c and r). n=8192, r size 32768. Could be okay. To reduce, can compute r via loops over active peaks for hard/soft with few peaks. But let's keep FFT. 2400s maybe 10k-50k iterations? FFT 32768 maybe 0.005s? Actually numpy rfft 32768 maybe ~0.01s; irfft 0.01; two conv maybe 0.04. 10k iter 400s. Plus exact. Good.

Could combine c and r? c uses FFT of a size 16384/32768; r uses product A*W_rev. Need both. Fine.

We also compute c_new after update: another FFT. So ~3 FFT/it. Maybe slower. Could use hard active weights and loop r to avoid r FFT, then one c FFT. Update rule (multiplicative):
```

   dir = r_center/scale
   v = 0.85*v + 0.15*dir
   a = a * np.exp(-lr*v)

```

Optimize function pseudocode:
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, seed=0, label=''):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize(a0,n)
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_exact=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
    best_fast=fast_score(a)
    v=np.zeros(n)
    lr=0.05
    beta=10.0
    K=3
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    last_exact=best_exact
    initial_time=...
    while time < deadline-10:
        it+=1
        c=fft_conv_full(a)  # can store c from previous? Let's store after update; initialize.
        cmax=c.max()
        fast_s=2*cmax/n
        d=c/n
        r=compute_r_top(a,d,K,beta)
        # update direction
        r_mean=r.mean()
        rc=r-r_mean
        scale=np.max(np.abs(rc))
        if scale>1e-8:
            dir=rc/scale
            v=0.85*v+0.15*dir
            step=np.exp(np.clip(-lr*v, -3, 3))
            a_new=a*step
            a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
            S=a_new.sum()
            if S<1e-12: a_new=best_a.copy()
            else: a_new=a_new*S? normalize: a_new *= n/S
        else:
            a_new=a.copy()
        # compute c_new and fast
        c_new=fft_conv_full(a_new)
        s_new=2*c_new.max()/n
        if s_new < best_fast or s_new < best_exact - 0.0005 or it%20==0:
            exact=evaluate_sequence(a_new.tolist())
            if finite and exact<best_exact:
                best_exact=exact; best_a=a_new.copy(); last_exact=best_exact; no_improve=0; print
                lr=min(lr*1.05,0.3)
            else: no_improve+=1
        else:
            no_improve += 1? maybe count fast nonimprove
        accept? Let's decide accept a_new unless reject condition.
        if s_new > fast_s*1.3 and no_improve>50:
            a_new=best_a.copy(); c_new=fft_conv_full(a); s_new=2*c_new.max()/n; lr*=0.7
        else:
            a=a_new
        c=c_new? if accepted; else c old.
        # adjust hyperparams
        if no_improve % 50 ==0:
             K = min(32, K*2)
             beta=min(500,beta*1.2)
             lr=max(0.005,lr*0.8)
        if no_improve > 300:
             a=best_a*exp(rng.normal(0,0.08,n)); normalize; v=zero; lr=0.05; beta=20; K=3; no_improve=0
        if it % 500 ==0: print progress
```

    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-8: return ones
    # scale up while max would exceed?

Compute top K efficiently:
```
def top_r(p, d, K, beta):
    n=len(p); L=2n-1
    if K >= L: order=np.arange(L)
    else: order=np.argpartition(d, -K)[-K:]
    order=order[np.argsort(d[order])[::-1]]
    vals=d[order]
    x=beta*(vals-vals[0])
    x -= x.max() # max 0
    w=np.exp(x)
    sw=w.sum()
    if sw <=0: w=np.zeros_like; w[0]=1
    else: w/=sw
    r=np.zeros(n)
    for k, wk in zip(order, w):
       left = k - (n-1)
       if left < 0: left = 0
       right = k
       if right >= n: right = n-1
       if right >= left:
          js = k - left
          je = k - right
          r[left:right+1] += wk * p[je:js+1][::-1]
    return r
```
Check slice for k > n: e.g., n=10,k=12: left=3,right=9. js=9? k-left=9, je=3. p[3:10][::-1] maps i=3 j=9, i=9 j=3. good.

```
def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, rng_seed, restart_seqs=None, tag=''):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    n=len(p0)
    p=normalize_sum(p0,1.0)
    p0_score=evaluate_sequence(p.tolist())
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=p0_score if finite else inf
    p_current=p.copy()
    c=fft_conv_full(p_current)
    d = n*c
    dmax=d.max(); cur_score=2*dmax
    best_fast=cur_score
    v=np.zeros(n)
    lr=0.12
    beta=15.0
    K=6
    momentum=0.80
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    seed_idx=0
    last_exact=best_score
    # if p0_score inf, set uniform
    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
       it+=1
       # d already from c; dmax d
       # compute r for top K
       r = compute_top_r(p_current, d, K, beta)
       rs = n*r
       rc = rs - rs.mean()
       scale = np.max(np.abs(rc))
       if scale > 1e-10:
           dir = rc/scale
           v = momentum*v + (1-momentum)*dir
           mult = np.exp(np.clip(-lr*v, -3.0, 3.0))
           p_new = p_current * mult
           S=p_new.sum()
           if S<1e-300 or not finite: p_new=best_p.copy()
           else: p_new /= S
           # safety max? values sum1 so max<=1; fine
       else:
           p_new = p_current.copy()
       c_new=fft_conv_full(p_new)
       d_new=n*c_new
       dmax_new=d_new.max()
       s_new=2*dmax_new
       # accept?
       accepted = s_new <= cur_score + 1e-6
       # if not and it < 200? allow some? Use allow if s_new < cur_score + 0.005 and no_improve>50
       if not accepted and no_improve > 50 and s_new < cur_score + 0.002:
           accepted=True
       if accepted:
           p_current=p_new; c=c_new; d=d_new; cur_score=s_new; dmax=dmax_new
           if s_new < best_fast:
               best_fast=s_new
               no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve +=1
       else:
           # reject
           lr *= 0.75
           v *= 0.5
           no_improve +=1
       # exact check
       if (it % 20 == 0 or s_new < last_exact - 0.002 or s_new < best_fast) and time.time()<deadline-10:
           exact = float(evaluate_sequence(p_current.tolist()))
           if np.isfinite(exact):
               if exact < best_score:
                   best_score=exact; best_p=p_current.copy(); last_exact=best_score; no_improve=0
                   lr = min(0.4, lr*1.1)
                   print(...)
               else:
                   no_improve +=1
           # if current much worse than best, maybe reset from best
           if cur_score > best_score + 0.05 and no_improve>100:
               p_current=best_p.copy()
               c=fft_conv_full(p_current); d=n*c; cur_score=2*d.max(); v=zeros; no_improve=0
       # adaptive hyper
       if no_improve % 100 == 0 and no_improve>0:
           if K < 24: K *= 2
           if beta < 200: beta *= 1.25
           lr = max(0.02, lr*0.8)
       if no_improve > 500:
           # restart
           if restart_seqs and seed_idx < len(restart_seqs):
               p_current=normalize_sum(restart_seqs[seed_idx],1.0); seed_idx+=1
           else:
               # random around best
               p_current = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25,n))
               p_current=normalize_sum(p_current,1.0)
           c=fft_conv_full(p_current); d=n*c; cur_score=2*d.max(); v=zeros; lr=0.12; beta=15; K=6; no_improve=0
       if it % 1000 ==0: print(f"{tag} it {it} fast {cur_score:.6f} best {best_score:.6f}")
       if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
    final exact best
    return best_p, best_score
```

Seed generation details with p sum1:
```
def normalize_sum(arr, target):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
   S=arr.sum()
   if not finite or S<1e-12:
      return np.full(len(arr), target/len(arr))
   return arr*(target/S)
```
`make_seeds(n, parent, seed=1):`

```
list=[]
t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
def add(f): f=np.maximum(f,0); if f.sum()>0: list.append(normalize_sum(f,1))
# uniform
add(np.ones(n))
# exponential ramps
for a in np.linspace(-5,5,9): add(np.exp(a*t))
# quadratics
for b in [-4,-2,0,2,4]: for a in [-2,0,2]: add(np.exp(a*(t-0.5)+b*(t-0.5)**2))
# sin/cos
for m in [1,2,3,4]: for amp in [1,2]: for ph in [0,0.1,0.25,0.5]: add(1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t+ph))
# gaussian humps
for p1 in [0.2,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.8]: for s in [0.08,0.15,0.25]: add(np.exp(-((t-p1)**2)/(2*s*s)))
# two humps
for p1,p2 in [(0.25,0.75),(0.2,0.8),(0.35,0.65)]: for s in [0.08,0.15,0.25]: add(exp(-(t-p1)^2/...)+exp(-(t-p2)^2/...))

for s in [0.08,0.15,0.25]: add(1 + np.exp(-((t-0.5)^2)/(2*s*s)))
# sawtooth/ periodic low freq: add(1+amp*np.sin(2π t+ph)) etc
```

def fast_score_p(p):
    S=p.sum(); n=len(p); if S<1e-12: inf
    c=fft_conv_full(p); return 2.0*n*c.max()/(S*S)

Implementing full r:
```
def compute_r_full(p, d, beta):
   n=len(p); L=2*n-1; dmax=d.max()
   x=beta*(d-dmax); x-=x.max(); e=np.exp(x); sw=e.sum(); if sw==0 ...; w=e/sw
   # W_rev = w[::-1]
   size=1; while size < n+L-1: size*=2
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size); fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],size)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,size)
   r=h[L-1 : L-n-1 : -1]
   return r
```

`fft_conv_full(a)`:
```
def fft_conv_full(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    size=1
    target=2*n-1
    while size < target:
        size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    c=c[:target]
    if (c < 0).any(): c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
`fast_score(p)`:
```
def fast_score(p):
    n=len(p); S=p.sum()
    if S <=0: return inf
    c=fft_conv_full(p)
    return float(2.0*n*c.max()/(S*S))
```
`compute_top_r` as above. Use `float` for beta.

Define:
```
def add_seed(list_vec, f):
    arr = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0: return
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    S = arr.sum()
    if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S): return
    arr = arr / S
    # ensure no inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return
    list_vec.append(arr.copy())
```
Then in make_seeds call `add_seed(seeds, expr)`. No nested function.

Resample parent:
```
def resample_to_length(x, n):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    x=np.clip(x,0,1e12)
    if x.size==n: return x
    if x.size==0: return ones
    xp=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,x.size)
    if x.size>1:
       t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       y=np.interp(t,xp,x)
    else: y=np.full(n,x[0])
    return np.maximum(y,0.0)
```

def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, rng_seed, restart_list=None, tag=''):
    start=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    n=len(p0)
    p=normalize_sum(p0,1.0)
    exact0=float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
    if not np.isfinite(exact0): p=np.ones(n)/n; exact0=evaluate...
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=exact0
    c=fft_conv_full(p)
    d=n*c
    cur_score=2*d.max()
    best_fast=cur_score
    v=np.zeros(n)
    lr=0.12
    beta=12.0
    K=4
    momentum=0.8
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    last_reset=0
    final_exact_best=best_score
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
        it += 1
        use_full = (no_improve > 200 and K >= 16 and (it % 40 == 0))
        if use_full:
            r = compute_r_full(p, d, max(2.0, beta*0.25))
        else:
            r = compute_top_r(p, d, K, beta)
        r_scaled = n * r
        rm = float(np.mean(r_scaled))
        rc = r_scaled - rm
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(rc)))
        if scale > 1e-12:
            dir = rc / scale
            v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * dir
            mult = np.exp(np.clip(-lr * v, -4.0, 4.0))
            p_new = p * mult
            p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0.0)
            # floor
            if p_new.min() <= 1e-15:
                # But if min already > floor no need; vector operations
                p_new = p_new + 1e-12
            S = float(p_new.sum())
            if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
                p_new = best_p.copy()
            else:
                p_new /= S
        else:
            p_new = p.copy()
        c_new = fft_conv_full(p_new)
        d_new = n * c_new
        s_new = float(2.0 * np.max(d_new))
        # accept monotone
        if s_new <= cur_score + 1e-8:
            accepted = True
        elif cur_score > best_score + 0.05 and s_new <= cur_score - 0.01:
            accepted = True  # improve current even if not global? covered by first
        else:
            accepted = False
        if accepted:
            p = p_new; c = c_new; d = d_new; cur_score = s_new
            if s_new < best_fast:
                best_fast = s_new
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            lr = max(0.02, lr * 0.75)
            v *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
        # exact check
        do_exact = (it % 25 == 0) or (s_new < last? no variable) or (s_new < best_fast) or (cur_score < best_score - 0.002)
```

Let's write `compute_top_r` robust:
```
def compute_top_r(p, d, K, beta):
    n = len(p); L = len(d)
    r = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0 or n ==0: return r
    if K < 1: K=1
    if K >= L:
       order = np.arange(L)
    else:
       order = np.argpartition(d, -K)[-K:]
       order = order[np.argsort(d[order])[::-1]]
    vals = d[order]
    v0 = vals[0]
    x = beta*(vals - v0)
    # x <=0, max 0
    x = np.maximum(x, -745.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    sw = w.sum()
    if sw <= 0 or not finite:
       w = np.zeros_like(w); w[0]=1; sw=1
    else: w /= sw
    for idx in range(order.size):
       k=int(order[idx]); wk=float(w[idx])
       if wk ==0: continue
       left = k - (n-1)
       if left < 0: left = 0
       right = k
       if right >= n: right = n-1
       if right >= left:
           je = k - right
           js = k - left
           # p[je:js+1]
           r[left:right+1] += wk * p[je:js+1][::-1]
    return r
```
Note if k large, k-left maybe <=n-1? left max ensures j start ≤n-1. Good.

`compute_r_full`:
```
def compute_r_full(p, d, beta):
    n=len(p); L=2*n-1
    r=np.zeros(n)
    if n==0: return r
    dmax=float(d.max())
    x=beta*(d-dmax)
    x=np.maximum(x,-745.0)
    e=np.exp(x)
    sw=e.sum()
    if sw <=0 or not finite:
       w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(d))]=1.0
    else:
       w=e/sw
    size=1
    target=n+L-1
    while size<target: size <<=1
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],size)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fp*fw,size)
    start=L-1
    stop=L-n-1
    if stop < -1: stop=-1
    r = h[start:stop:-1]
    # ensure length n; if mismatch pad
    if r.size != n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n)
       m=min(n, r.size); tmp[:m]=r[:m]; r=tmp
    r=np.maximum(r,0)
    return r
```
Check stop for n=1: L=1, target=1? n+L-1=1, size1, start0, stop=1-1-1=-1. h[0:-1:-1]? empty. But n≥large. For n=1 handle earlier. In general L-n-1 = (2n-1)-n-1 = n-2; stop n-2 -> start 2n-2 down to 0? Wait L-1=2n-2, L-n-1=n-2; slice `h[2n-2:n-2:-1]` yields indices 2n-2 down to n-1? Need desired indices m=L-1-i, i=0..n-1 -> m=2n-2,2n-3,...,n-1. Stop exclusive should be n-2, correct. Good.
But `h` length size ≥ n+L-1=3n-2, so indices valid. r length n. `r = h[L-1:L-n-1:-1]` works if L-n-1 >=0; for n=1 stop=-1, then `h[0:-1:-1]` empty; but not.
For n=2: L=3, L-n-1=0, start2 stop0 -> indices2,1 length2. desired i=0 m=2, i=1 m=1. good.

Let's write `make_seeds` top-level:
```
def make_seeds(n, parent, limit=100):
    seeds=[]
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    u=t-0.5
    add_seed(seeds,parent)
    add_seed(seeds,np.ones(n))
    # exponential ramps
    for a in (-5.0,-3.0,-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0,5.0):
       add_seed(seeds, np.exp(a*t))
       add_seed(seeds, np.exp(-a*t))
    # quadratic
    for b in (-4.0,-2.0,0.0,2.0,4.0):
       for a in (-3.0,-1.5,0.0,1.5,3.0):
          add_seed(seeds, np.exp(a*u + b*u*u))
    # sinusoidal
    for m in (1,2,3,4):
       for amp in (1.0,2.0):
          for ph in (0.0,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75):
             z=1.0 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + ph)
             add_seed(seeds,z)
             add_seed(seeds, np.exp(z))
    # gaussian single
    for pos in (0.1,0.25,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.75,0.9):
       for s in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35):
          add_seed(seeds, np.exp(-((t-pos)**2)/(2.0*s*s)))
    # two humps
    for p1,p2 in ((0.2,0.8),(0.25,0.75),(0.3,0.7),(0.35,0.65),(0.15,0.85)):
       for s in (0.05,0.1,0.2):
          z=np.exp(-((t-p1)**2)/(2*s*s)) + np.exp(-((t-p2)**2)/(2*s*s))
          add_seed(seeds,z)
    # broad valley/peak
    for s in (0.1,0.2,0.35):
       add_seed(seeds, 1.0 + 2.0*np.exp(-((t-0.5)**2)/(2*s*s)))
       add_seed(seeds, 1.0 - 0.5*np.exp(-((t-0.5)**2)/(2*s*s)))
    # smooth random noise from deterministic rng
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    if parent.size == n and parent.sum()>0:
       base=parent
    else:
       base=np.ones(n)
    for sig in (0.2,0.5,1.0):
       noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n))
       add_seed(seeds, base*noise)
    # If too many, truncate deterministic perhaps
    if len(seeds) > limit:
       seeds = seeds[:limit]
    return seeds
```
This may create many high oscillatory exp(z) with z=1+2cos could be zero? add handles. Limit 100; random at end might cut if many before? We trunc after all, maybe random included if len>limit? If len>limit, take first 100, may cut random. Let's cap by adding random before truncation; if many parametric, random may be cut. ```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative step heights for

    score(a) = 2 * len(a) * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

using a soft-max / natural-gradient multiplicative optimizer.

The algorithm:
  * normalizes candidates to sum 1 (score is scale invariant),
  * uses FFT to compute A*A and approximate the max by a softmax over
    selected peak coefficients,
  * updates coefficients multiplicatively in log-space using a
    natural gradient direction p_i * (sum over peak shifts of p_shift),
  * accepts moves that do not increase the fast FFT score,
  * periodically validates with the exact evaluate_sequence,
  * performs simulated restarts from diverse seeds and from the incumbent
    best sequence.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np


def next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fft_conv_full(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)

    target = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(target)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    c = c[:target]
    if np.any(c < 0.0):
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def fast_score(p):
    """Fast FFT score for arbitrary nonnegative p."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf
    c = fft_conv_full(p)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()) / (S * S))


def normalize_sum(a, target=1.0):
    """Clip to nonnegative and scale to the requested sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.full(a.shape, target / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a *= float(target) / S
    return a


def resample_to_length(x, n):
    """Linearly resample a sequence to length n, preserving shape."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0e12, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)

    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == n:
        return x
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(x[0]), dtype=np.float64)

    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, x.size)
    tp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(tp, xp, x)
    return np.maximum(y, 0.0)


def add_seed(list_vec, f):
    """Append a normalized nonnegative seed if it is valid."""
    arr = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return
    arr = arr / S
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return
    list_vec.append(arr.copy())


def make_seeds(n, parent, limit=260):
    """Generate a diverse set of initial seeds of length n."""
    seeds = []
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = t - 0.5

    # The parent and uniform reference.
    add_seed(seeds, parent)
    add_seed(seeds, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Exponential ramps.
    for a in (-5.0, -3.0, -2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        add_seed(seeds, np.exp(a * t))
        add_seed(seeds, np.exp(-a * t))

    # Quadratic exponentials.
    for b in (-4.0, -2.5, -1.0, 1.0, 2.5, 4.0):
        for a in (-3.0, -1.5, 0.0, 1.5, 3.0):
            add_seed(seeds, np.exp(a * u + b * u * u))

    # Periodic shapes.
    for m in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        for amp in (1.0, 2.0):
            for ph in (0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
                z = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t + ph)
                add_seed(seeds, z)
                add_seed(seeds, np.exp(z))

    # Single Gaussian humps.
    for pos in (0.1, 0.2, 0.25, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.75, 0.8, 0.9):
        for s in (0.05, 0.1, 0.18, 0.3):
            add_seed(seeds, np.exp(-((t - pos) ** 2) / (2.0 * s * s)))

    # Two broad humps.
    for p1, p2 in ((0.15, 0.85), (0.2, 0.8), (0.25, 0.75),
                   (0.3, 0.7), (0.35, 0.65)):
        for s in (0.05, 0.1, 0.18):
            z = (np.exp(-((t - p1) ** 2) / (2.0 * s * s)) +
                 np.exp(-((t - p2) ** 2) / (2.0 * s * s)))
            add_seed(seeds, z)

    # Central valley / broad peak shapes.
    for s in (0.1, 0.18, 0.3):
        add_seed(seeds, 1.0 + 2.0 * np.exp(-((t - 0.5) ** 2) / (2.0 * s * s)))
        add_seed(seeds, 1.0 - 0.5 * np.exp(-((t - 0.5) ** 2) / (2.0 * s * s)))
        add_seed(seeds, np.exp(1.0 - 2.0 * np.exp(-((t - 0.5) ** 2) / (2.0 * s * s))))

    # Deterministic smooth parent noise.
    rng = np.random.default_rng(123456)
    base = parent if (parent.size == n and float(parent.sum()) > 0) else np.ones(n)
    for sig in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
        noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n))
        add_seed(seeds, base * noise)

    if len(seeds) > limit:
        seeds = seeds[:limit]
    return seeds


def compute_top_r(p, d, K, beta):
    """
    Compute r_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i} for the top K coefficients d_k,
    where w is a softmax over those K peaks.
    """
    n = len(p)
    L = len(d)
    r = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return r

    if K < 1:
        K = 1
    if K >= L:
        order = np.arange(L)
    else:
        order = np.argpartition(d, -K)[-K:]
        order = order[np.argsort(d[order])[::-1]]

    vals = d[order]
    v0 = float(vals[0])
    x = beta * (vals - v0)
    x = np.maximum(x, -745.0)
    w = np.exp(x)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w = np.zeros_like(w, dtype=np.float64)
        w[0] = 1.0
        sw = 1.0
    else:
        w /= sw

    for idx in range(order.size):
        wk = float(w[idx])
        if wk <= 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(order[idx])
        left = k - (n - 1)
        if left < 0:
            left = 0
        right = k
        if right >= n:
            right = n - 1
        if right >= left:
            je = k - right
            js = k - left
            r[left:right + 1] += wk * p[je:js + 1][::-1]

    return r


def compute_r_full(p, d, beta):
    """
    Compute r_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i} using softmax weights over all lags.
    More expensive but useful as a periodic regularization move.
    """
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    r = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0:
        return r

    dmax = float(d.max())
    x = beta * (d - dmax)
    x = np.maximum(x, -745.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    sw = float(e.sum())
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(d))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / sw

    target = n + L - 1
    size = next_pow2(target)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, size)

    start = L - 1
    stop = L - n - 1
    r = h[start:stop:-1]
    if r.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(n, r.size)
        tmp[:m] = r[:m]
        r = tmp

    r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
    return r


def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, rng_seed, restarts=None, tag="opt"):
    """
    Run the main multiplicative natural-gradient search.
    Returns (best sequence, exact best score).
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    n = len(p0)

    p = normalize_sum(p0, 1.0)
    exact0 = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
    if not np.isfinite(exact0):
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        exact0 = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
        if not np.isfinite(exact0):
            exact0 = 2.0

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = exact0

    c = fft_conv_full(p)
    d = float(n) * c
    cur_score = float(2.0 * d.max())
    best_fast = cur_score
    last_exact = best_score
    last_exact_it = 0

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    lr = 0.08
    max_lr = 0.35
    min_lr = 0.02
    momentum = 0.80

    K = 4
    beta = 12.0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    if restarts is None:
        restart_q = []
    else:
        restart_q = [normalize_sum(x, 1.0) for x in restarts]

    exact_gap = 70

    while True:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 8.0:
            break

        it += 1

        use_full = (no_improve > 250 and K >= 12 and (it % 64 == 0))
        if use_full:
            r = compute_r_full(p, d, max(2.0, beta * 0.25))
        else:
            r = compute_top_r(p, d, K, beta)

        rs = float(n) * r
        g = p * rs
        gm = float(np.mean(g))
        gc = g - gm
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(gc)))

        if scale > 1e-12:
            direction = gc / scale
            v = momentum * v + (1.0 - momentum) * direction
            mult = np.exp(np.clip(-lr * v, -4.0, 4.0))
            p_new = p * mult
            p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0.0)

            # tiny floor so zero entries can later receive mass
            p_new += 1e-12
            S = float(p_new.sum())
            if S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
                p_new = best_p.copy()
            else:
                p_new /= S
        else:
            p_new = p.copy()

        c_new = fft_conv_full(p_new)
        d_new = float(n) * c_new
        s_new = float(2.0 * float(d_new.max()))

        # Monotone fast-score acceptance, with a small tolerance when stuck.
        tol = 1e-8
        if no_improve > 200:
            tol = 2e-4
        accepted = (s_new <= cur_score + tol) or (s_new < best_score - 5e-4)

        if accepted:
            p = p_new
            c = c_new
            d = d_new
            cur_score = s_new
            if s_new < best_fast:
                best_fast = s_new
                no_improve = 0
                lr = min(max_lr, lr * 1.03)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            lr = max(min_lr, lr * 0.72)
            v *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1

        # Periodic exact validation.
        if accepted and (
            (it - last_exact_it >= exact_gap) or (s_new < best_score - 0.01)
        ) and (deadline - time.monotonic() > 12.0):
            last_exact_it = it
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence(p.tolist()))
            if np.isfinite(exact):
                if exact < best_score:
                    best_score = exact
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    last_exact = best_score
                    no_improve = 0
                    lr = min(max_lr, lr * 1.08)
                    if it % 400 == 0 or best_score < 1.55:
                        print(f"{tag} it={it} exact={best_score:.10f} fast={cur_score:.10f}")
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

        # Ad几ust hyperparameters if stalled.
        if no_improve > 0 and no_improve % 90 == 0:
            if K < 24:
                K = min(24, K * 2)
            beta = min(350.0, beta * 1.18)
            lr = max(min_lr, lr * 0.85)

        # Restart if clearly stuck.
        if no_improve > 800:
            if len(restart_q) > 0:
                p = restart_q.pop(0)
            else:
                p = best_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.28, n))
                p += rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, n) * 1e-6
                p = normalize_sum(p, 1.0)

            c = fft_conv_full(p)
            d = float(n) * c
            cur_score = float(2.0 * d.max())
            best_fast = cur_score
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            lr = 0.08
            beta = 12.0
            K = 4
            no_improve = 0
            last_exact = best_score
            last_exact_it = it

            print(f"{tag} restart it={it} cur_fast={cur_score:.10f} best={best_score:.10f}")

        if it % 2500 == 0:
            print(f"{tag} it={it} cur_fast={cur_score:.10f} best_exact={best_score:.10f} K={K} beta={beta:.2f} lr={lr:.4f}")

    # Final validation of the local best.
    final_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(best_p.tolist()))
    if np.isfinite(final_exact) and final_exact < best_score:
        best_score = final_exact
    elif not np.isfinite(final_exact):
        best_p = p
        best_score = float(evaluate_sequence(best_p.tolist()))

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point. Returns a list of nonnegative floats minimizing the score.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    if budget_s is None or not np.isfinite(budget_s) or float(budget_s) <= 10.0:
        budget_s = min(2350.0, 2400.0)
    deadline = start + float(budget_s) - 35.0

    # Parent sequence, if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or parent.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
        parent = np.ones(2535, dtype=np.float64)

    parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
    parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)

    if float(parent.sum()) <= 1e-12:
        parent = np.ones(2535, dtype=np.float64)

    # Normalize parent to sum 1 for comparison/return.
    p_parent = normalize_sum(parent, 1.0)
    best_score = float(evaluate_sequence(p_parent.tolist()))
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        p_parent = np.full(8192, 1.0 / 8192.0, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = float(evaluate_sequence(p_parent.tolist()))
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            best_score = 2.0

    best_p = p_parent.copy()
    print(f"initial best={best_score:.10f} n={best_p.size}")

    # Work at a larger length for better continuous-difference approximation.
    n = 8192
    parent_res = normalize_sum(resample_to_length(p_parent, n), 1.0)
    parent_res_score = float(evaluate_sequence(parent_res.tolist()))
    if np.isfinite(parent_res_score) and parent_res_score < best_score:
        best_score = parent_res_score
        best_p = parent_res.copy()
        print(f"resampled parent improved best={best_score:.10f} n={n}")

    if deadline - time.monotonic() < 200.0:
        final = normalize_sum(best_p, 1.0)
        fs = float(evaluate_sequence(final.tolist()))
        if np.isfinite(fs):
            best_score = fs
            best_p = final
        return [float(x) for x in best_p]

    # Generate and score seeds.
    seeds = make_seeds(n, parent_res)
    scored = []
    for idx, s in enumerate(seeds):
        s2 = normalize_sum(s, 1.0)
        sc = fast_score(s2)
        scored.append((sc, idx, s2))

    scored.sort()

    # Candidate set: best fast seeds plus parent-resampled.
    candidates = [p for _, _, p in scored[:8]]
    candidates.append(parent_res)

    ranked = []
    for p in candidates:
        p2 = normalize_sum(p, 1.0)
        es = float(evaluate_sequence(p2.tolist()))
        if not np.isfinite(es):
            es = fast_score(p2)
            if not np.isfinite(es):
                es = 1e9
        ranked.append((es, len(ranked), p2))

    ranked.sort()

    if len(ranked) == 0:
        initial_p = parent_res
        restarts = []
    else:
        initial_p = ranked[0][2]
        restarts = [p for _, _, p in ranked[1:8]]
        # Also add parent as a final restart.
        restarts.append(parent_res)

    # Main optimization.
    opt_seq, opt_score = optimize_sequence(
        initial_p,
        deadline,
        rng_seed=seed,
        restarts=restarts,
        tag="main"
    )

    if np.isfinite(opt_score) and opt_score < best_score:
        best_score = opt_score
        best_p = opt_seq
        print(f"optimizer best={best_score:.10f} n={best_p.size}")

    # If the exponentalized parent retained a better score and we did not beat it,
    # still finish from the current best once more for a short polishing phase.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 120.0:
        pol_score = float(evaluate_sequence(best_p.tolist()))
        if not np.isfinite(pol_score):
            pol_score = best_score
        if pol_score < best_score:
            best_score = pol_score
            best_p = normalize_sum(best_p, 1.0)

        polish_seq, polish_score = optimize_sequence(
            normalize_sum(best_p, 1.0),
            deadline,
            rng_seed=seed + 17,
            restarts=[parent_res],
            tag="polish"
        )
        if np.isfinite(polish_score) and polish_score < best_score:
            best_score = polish_score
            best_p = polish_seq

    # Final validation.
    final_p = normalize_sum(best_p, 1.0)
    final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(final_p.tolist()))
    if np.isfinite(final_score) and final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score
        best_p = final_p

    print(f"final best={best_score:.10f} n={best_p.size}")
    return [float(x) for x in best_p]
```
